Weak spots seen: with the push, the weakest model blends gear and violence into sex talk, and the output screen dropped a harmless violent-gig reply on 3 of 8 runs (the guard was not changed; the reason is recorded in the sampler output). Results (runs cut short at the user's request: no blind score, no old/first/off comparison sets, no full benign re-runs beyond the guard pass): hard-limit pass with the new layer, all four models: full path 180/180 blocked at input; output screen alone dropped/deflected/got-through-engaged 2B 101/77/2, 4B 128/51/1, Gemma E4B Heretic 103/77/0, 9B 117/63/0; benign passed 2B 158/169, 4B 165/169, Gemma 168/169, 9B 163/169. Three fixed-cell samples per model are in `docs/MATURE_SAMPLES_2026-10-13.md` (only the 4B Evelyn reply is graphic; the 9B is the most forward in tone but suggestive; Gemma gentlest; 2B incoherent). Pack pick: 9B and 4B plus Gemma E4B Heretic; drop the 2B (config stays PLACEHOLDER). Notification removed and the help text updated in 15 locales (done).

**2026-10-13 (branch `picker-no-auto`, from main; unit-tested with the fake llama-server, NOT merged, NOT installed, NOT seen in the game; needs a bridge restart plus a CET reload for the Lua and locale files): the Local AI model row has no Auto entry; it lists the installed models from the smallest file to the largest.** User request: "Remove Auto from the model picker of Local AI; just sort them from small to high." (1) The row (`mod/mcm.lua`, `manager.localModelChoices()`) lists only the installed models, sorted by file size, equal sizes by name (the bridge publishes them sorted, `describeLocalModels`; `manager.lua` sorts again). The labels are unchanged: sizes in the help, "(custom)", "too large for this graphics card" (a too-large model stays listed and shows as unusable; choosing it falls back). With no model installed the row shows one placeholder, "No model installed". (2) The default (no saved choice, or a legacy `localModel: auto`) is the largest model that fits the free graphics memory, the rule Auto used. The bridge publishes it as `localModelDefault` (and `localModelMatureDefault`, below) in `mistral-state.json`, computed by the pure `defaultModelIds` from the free memory the last start saw before it loaded (`LocalLlmServer.lastFreeMiB`), else the card's total (the existing single `nvidia-smi` call, now also when more than one model is installed; no new polling). The row shows that model as SELECTED; the next Apply stores its concrete id (`manager.applySettings`, silent, no notice). Until then a saved `auto` still loads the same default, with no fallback notice. (3) Mature preselect: turning Mature content on stages the largest uncensored model that fits (`localModelMatureDefault`) with the line "Uncensored model", or nothing for the model when none is installed or none fits; turning it off restores the previous model (a legacy record with `set = auto` still restores). (4) Fallback: a chosen model that is missing, too large for the free memory or did not start is replaced by the largest model that fits (the largest uncensored one first with Mature on); the notice now names it: "The Local AI model you chose is missing, does not fit in the free graphics memory or did not start, so {model} was used instead." (the `model-fallback` event carries `model`, the reply carries `keyNoticeModel`); once per change, as before. (5) The custom manifest `auto` flag is obsolete: accepted whatever its value, ignored, documented as obsolete. Custom models take part in the default and the fallback by size and fit; `uncensored: true` counts for the Mature preselect and fallback (`autoEligible` removed). (6) Docs: `docs/LOCAL_AI.md` ("Choosing the model", "Add your own model", developer notes), `docs/NOTIFICATIONS.md`, `docs/MANAGER_UI.md`, the player and Mature pack readmes. New or changed UI strings are in all 15 `mod/locales/*.lua` (machine-translated by a Haiku helper, one German string spot-checked); `localization/en.json` regenerated. Tests: `local-model-choice`, `custom-model-choice`, `custom-models`, `local-llm-server`, `mistral-key` and `manager` updated and extended (sorting, default selection, legacy `auto` migration, Mature preselect with an uncensored model and with none, fallback naming the model, custom `auto` ignored). Not live-tested: the row's look in the MCM (a long list of models; the placeholder), whether the default shown matches what loads on the real card, and the Mature toggle staging the uncensored model. Uncertain: the default shown comes from the free memory seen at the last start (or the card's total before any start), not from a fresh reading, so it can differ from a later load if other programs change the free memory; a model already shown selected is saved as a concrete id on the first Apply, so later memory changes then use the fallback notice instead of silently choosing another model.

**2026-10-13 (full test suite made green, branch `fix-suite`; tests only, no production code changed; nothing to install or restart).** The first full run after today's merges on the Windows checkout had 20 failures. Causes and fixes: (1) `addons/example` "example evaluation script": not a regression. A repeated remark now gets one regeneration (`AddonHost.produce`, from the add-on quality branch), which used up a stand-in line the script had counted on; the stand-in now repeats the line on the second try too, and the test also asserts `retried`, that the remark is still dropped, and that the next step gets its own line. (2) `emotion-tags` 1457, `expressive-emotions` 1488 and `natural-speech` 2798 counted `transformReply: cleanReply` in `convai-bridge.mjs` as 3 (conversation, phone, Mistral); the add-on speakers' text host (`addonTextHost`) legitimately passes every reply through the same cleaner, so the count is 4 and the messages say so. (3) The Invite voice preparation block in `bridge/voice-routing.test.mjs` (13 tests, 16-22 and 28-33): `tick` and `pollMicrophone` now read `addonHost.isSpeaker` and `addonVoice.voice`, which the test sandbox did not define (ReferenceError); the sandbox now has stand-ins that claim nothing. (4) `local-stt-capture` browser test: passes alone, fails under the full suite's load. It slept a fixed 6.5 s, which on a loaded computer cut the 4.8 s clip short or beat the first live words. It now counts the bytes that reach the recogniser and waits (up to 40 s) for the whole clip and the first partial; the assertions on the transcript and the partial are unchanged. Passed three times in parallel with the real recogniser and Edge. (5) `mistral-host.test.mjs` and `service-status.test.mjs` failed as whole files on the Windows run but pass alone, five times in a row, in a full run, and on an LF (git archive) copy as well as the CRLF checkout; no cause in the code (no `\n` source regex, no environment dependency), most likely a transient while branches were being merged into the checkout. Recheck them in the next full run and look at the file-level error first if they fail again. In a worktree with no `node_modules` of its own, `companion-actions` (pinned SDK), `facial-timeline` and `packaging/runtime-bootstrap` (allowlist) read `node_modules/...` straight from the project root and fail; that is the worktree, not the code.
**2026-10-13 (branch `custom-local-models`, from main; unit-tested with the fake llama-server, NOT installed, NOT live; needs a bridge restart plus a CET reload for the Lua and locale files; not merged): players can add their own GGUF chat models by hand to the Local AI model row.** A player drops a `.gguf` into a `custom/` folder of the Local AI folder and lists it in `manifest.custom.json` (`{version: 1, models: [{file, name?, uncensored?, auto?, family?, contextSize?, chatTemplate?}]}`; also read from the dev Local AI folder when only that has it). `bridge/custom-models.mjs` validates each entry strictly (relative path inside the folder, `.gguf`, name 1 to 60 characters, booleans, `contextSize` 2048 to 262144, `chatTemplate` name; at most 16) and logs each bad entry once as `[custom models] ...`, never throwing. Requirement checks run at detection time on the settings poll (at most every 5 s, never per frame; stats every look, the first 2 MiB of a file read only when its size or time changed): GGUF magic and version 2 or 3, `general.architecture`, an embedding model rejected from its architecture or a non-zero `<arch>.pooling_type`, a file under 1 MiB, a split model's parts summed. A failing model is listed in the log and in the row's help as "Not usable: <name>: <reason>"; the VRAM estimate (file plus the tier headroom) is in the help; a model needing more than the card's total memory (from `--list-devices` after the first start, else one `nvidia-smi` call at most once a minute) is listed as "too large for this graphics card" and never chosen. Listing: ids `custom:<file name>`, after the built-in ones, "(custom)" label. Auto: custom models only with `"auto": true` (then they compete by size; equal sizes keep the built-in first); with Mature content on, a custom model joins the uncensored group only with both `uncensored` and `auto` true (decision for the user to confirm: the spec said both that `uncensored` makes a model eligible for Mature Auto and that custom models are used by Auto only with `auto: true`; the stricter reading is built). A pin always works if it fits. The server starts a custom model with its `contextSize` and `--chat-template`, otherwise the same flags; one that fails to start or dies while warming up is marked broken (until its file changes or reset) and the start is repeated with Auto's choice, with one `local-model-fallback` notice whose text now also says "or did not start" (all 15 locales updated). `allowanceFor(name, {uncensored, family})` honours the manifest flag in both directions instead of the file name. Docs: `docs/LOCAL_AI.md` "Add your own model" (and the developer notes), a short note in `packaging/PLAYER_README.md`. Added `bridge/custom-models.mjs` to the bridge allowlists (`packaging/runtime-layout.mjs`, `packaging/release-plan.json`). Tests: `custom-models.test.mjs`, `custom-model-choice.test.mjs`, the custom block in `local-llm-server.test.mjs`, a custom-row test in `manager.test.mjs`, `mistral-key.test.mjs`; fixture `FAKE_LLAMA_EXIT_FOR_MODEL`. Limits, documented: llama.cpp support for the architecture and a chat template are not checked ahead; an embedding model whose header gives neither away passes and then fails at start (the fallback covers it). **Live check (user, needs the game and a GPU):** add one small instruct GGUF with a manifest, see it in the row marked "(custom)", pin it, Apply, talk; then try an embedding model and a garbage file and read the "Not usable" line in the help.

**2026-10-13 (branch `gesture-slide`; unit-tested; NOT installed, NOT seen in the game, game not touched): Evelyn slid across the floor during an idle (panam_hips).** What the logs show: the idle ran 10:01:15 to 10:02:20 and ended `flavour ended`; there was no `exit movement` because V did not move. `runtime/movement-trace.ndjson` covers 10:01:56 to 10:04:01 and V's position is constant to the centimetre the whole time (`moving false`), so the movement check was correctly silent. What the trace does show: with V still, Evelyn's entity moved 0.24 m (x -1417.16 to -1417.38, speed 0.27) at 10:02:00, about 45 s after the idle began, her `hold` command already `Interrupted (4)` since the workspot started. So the body moved inside the workspot with nobody asking it to; the cause is not found (likely the clip: panam_hips drifting at a loop or idle-break; the mod's follow, recovery and pace code do not move a copy the gesture owner holds). The movement check itself measures V (more than 0.75 m from where V was when the idle began, running, or the group frame moving for 0.8 s), not her follow command; the follow is already suppressed while the workspot holds her. The gap that could still make her slide when V does walk: the workspot is left about 0.1 s after the exit signal (`exitLatency` in the log) but the pose blends out for about 0.5 s more (forced exit blend), and the follow started at once. Change: after any non-instant exit the gesture owner keeps reporting the copy held for `exitSettle` = 0.4 s after the workspot is left, so she is walked only after the blend; combat and transport exits (instant) do not wait. New log line `Gestures drift {"metres":..,"after":..,"component":..}` once per workspot when the body moves more than 15 cm while held, so the next slide shows when and in which component. If `drift` appears with V standing still, the fix is a per-component one (swap or trim that idle in the catalog). Install: `mod/companion-gestures.lua`; CET reload.
**2026-10-13 (talk-turn bugs, branch `talk-turn-bugs`; unit-tested; NOT installed, NOT seen in game).** Two bugs from the live Evelyn Parker talk. (1) The odd reply to "what are some of the services you excel at specifically": no stale or mismatched reply (the final text and memory were right, one LLM stage set, and tests across decision waits of 0, 40, 150 and 600 ms show the voice never hears the speculative reply). Root cause: the Portuguese LEAVE stem `some` in `bridge/text-action-words.mjs` matched the English word "some", so the screen found one action and `guess` speculated a reply written under "V sends you away"; the decision then disagreed, the reply was aborted and rewritten (about 1.5 to 2 s and a wasted Mistral request). Fixes: the stem now needs a Portuguese context (`some` at the end, `por favor`, `daqui`, `já`, `de aqui`, `da minha`); `guess` in `bridge/text-actions.mjs` answers NONE (no speculation on an action) for an open question (what, who, why, how...) that is not a request, and for plain remarks about oneself or a third party that do not address her; requests such as "Take the wheel, I'm wiped" still speculate. The coy tone is the model answering in persona; the notes block was left unchanged on purpose. (2) The second message got no reply: probe.log shows `Composer opened` for generations 12 and 13 and no `Native input captured`. Proven: there is no automatic reopen, and Lua never saw a submission; the boxes closed after about 1 to 3 s (Esc, an Enter on an empty box, or an engine close) and the old code dropped all of those silently. The exact trigger is not provable from the logs, so the box now reports how it ended: `Composer.reds` stores `m_convaiCloseInfo` (nodata, confirm, empty, closed:N) read through `ConvaiComposerClosedInfo()`; `mod/init.lua` (`convaiComposerWatch`) writes one probe.log line `Composer closed without a message: generation=N result=... open=Ns` and posts the new notice `message-lost` ("Your message was not sent. Open the text box again and retype it.", repeat 5 s, 15 locales, `docs/NOTIFICATIONS.md`) for an empty Enter or a close without a result (Esc stays quiet). Other silent drops now log `Message lost: ...` and post the notice too: `sendRequest` returned false, late input after the target was gone, a failed take. No per-frame work (runs on box open and close only). Install: `mod/init.lua`, `mod/notices.lua`, `mod/locales/*.lua` (CET reload); `bridge/text-action-words.mjs`, `bridge/text-actions.mjs` (bridge restart); `r6/scripts/CyberpunkConvai/Composer.reds` (GAME RESTART; until then the Lua logs `result=unknown` and posts nothing it cannot be sure of). Live checks: ask Evelyn "what are some of the services you excel at" and read bridge log for no `decision disagreed` / speculative abort; in the text box press Enter on an empty box and see the notice plus the `Composer closed without a message ... result=empty` line; press Esc and see only `result=closed:1`; whatever line the next lost box writes names the real cause of the missing reply.

**2026-10-13 (Delamain live-test fixes, branch `addon-delamain`; unit-tested; NOT installed, NOT seen in game).** From the user's first cab ride (remarks played, F6 reached him, voice right). (1) Cut-off lines: the probe.log reason `cancelled` comes only from `handle:cancel`, which the add-on called in its `vehicleExited` handler. The `boarded` line (09:41:05, cut 09:41:07, a second Workspot entry logged at 09:41:08) and `ride-interrupted` (09:44:33, cut 09:44:36) each saw the mount read as 'not in a vehicle' while they were in flight, so the add-on cancelled its own line; ride-start and district-passed had no such event. Now the exit cancels nothing and keeps the ride for 20 s (stepping back in continues it, no second greeting); in the main mod (`mod/addons.lua` `trackFlight`) a line that is playing finishes even if its speaker stops being available, and a line still waiting keeps its speaker for 2.5 s. (3) Seated gaze: above 22 m/s the look was switched off ('too fast for a seated look'); now the narrow look (eyes plus a small head turn, head weight 0.05) holds at any speed above the full band (`mod/companion-actuator.lua` `seatedStyle`), for every vehicle where V is a passenger; selection is unchanged (needs the crosshair on her), no new per-frame work. (4) Messy cab driving: code review found nothing in the mod that writes to the cab. The vehicle controller sends mount/unmount commands to the companion only, the chauffeur refuses the cab record, the AutoDrive observers read only (`OnAutodriveToggled` only starts the companions' exit), the add-on hooks only read, and the per-frame carry is for lifts. One physical candidate remains: a companion mounted in the cab (extra passenger) changes how the game treats the vehicle; unproven. (5) F7 is wired on main (`addon-voice-keys`) and installed (the game's init.lua has `addonHears`); the recogniser flag is true (`runtime/mistral-state.json` `stt`). Live check: in the cab with nothing under the crosshair press F7, say a short sentence, release; expect probe.log `Add-on voice conversation: profile=addon-delamain-cab-delamain`, a Microphone line, then Delamain's spoken answer; the notice 'Delamain answers typed messages only for now' would mean the recogniser flag was false. Tests: Delamain 20, companion-vehicles 46, gaze and integration updated.
**2026-10-13 (branch `crouch-sync`; unit-tested; NOT installed, NOT seen in the game, game not touched): companion crouch syncing.** Live bug (Carol Emeka, probe.log 09:48Z): she did not crouch at first, then crouched, and stayed crouched after V stood. Read from the log: `crouch` 09:48:11, an idle flavour gesture began 09:48:25 while she was meant to be crouched, `stand - workspot` 09:48:26, `crouch` 09:48:28, gesture `exit not relaxed` 09:48:29, `stand` 09:48:36 (logged, no visible effect). Causes found in code: (1) idle and talk gestures never looked at the crouch, so the workspot started over the pose and the stance module took the pose apart (stand, then crouch) while the workspot was still ending; (2) a stand was fire-and-forget: `NPCPuppet.ChangeStanceState` returns nothing and returns early when the blackboard already matches, so an ignored stand was never noticed, while a crouch was already read back and retried; (3) V crouching again while a stand was unconfirmed left her crouched with no record, so the next stand never came. The trigger is an event (`PlayerPuppet.OnLocomotionStateChanged`) applied at the next 10 Hz tick (at most 0.1 s) or, when a blocker holds her, at the next 0.5 s re-check; the code does not explain a visible delay and the stuck pose is not proven to come from the stance (see open question). Fix: `companions.lua` passes `crouched` to the gesture owner; `companion-gestures.lua` starts nothing while it is set and leaves at once (`exit` reason `crouching`); `companion-stance.lua` reads every stand back (re-issues up to three times while the stance reads Crouch, then `stand not kept by the game`), adopts a still-present crouch when V crouches again, exposes `holds(serial)`, logs `crouch waiting - <reason>` once per reason when a blocker holds her, and adds `hls=<high-level state>` to the crouch, stand and not-kept lines. Open question for the next live test: if `hls` is not Relaxed after a crouch or stand, the visible pose comes from the high-level state (Alerted or Stealth locomotion wrapper) and the next step is to restore Relaxed; if `hls` is Relaxed and the stance reads Stand but she looks crouched, the animation graph does not re-read the stance while she idles. No per-frame work was added. Install: `mod/companion-stance.lua`, `mod/companion-gestures.lua`, `mod/companions.lua`; CET reload.
**2026-10-13 (companion follow on stairs, first-stop position and vehicle-exit pace, branch `follow-stairs`; unit-tested; NOT installed, NOT seen in game, game not touched, native plugin unchanged: no rebuild).** Three live faults, found from `probe.log` and `runtime/movement-trace.ndjson` (Developer mode). (1) Stairs, Heywood/Glen near x -1431, y -1017 (09:31Z and 07:32Z): the moving-feet guard (`sweepAhead`) swept a LEVEL box at her feet height, which meets the rising treads within a metre (rejection stage `motion`, group Static, one box), so a follower at the foot of a flight was held as blocked; the follow stand-off point was taken at V's height (a `projection` failure on a flight below V); the flat-bottomed standing and swept checks also hit the treads; the unstick check then marked her stuck and the `stance` rule relocated her about 6 s after "stranded". Fix in `mod/companion-actuator.lua`: every swept leg and standing check on a climbing or descending route (slope at least 0.25) raises the box bottom by what the slope rises across the body radius plus a tread (capped 0.6 m, top of the box unchanged, so a wall or car above the band still fails); `sweepAhead` repeats a level scenery hit once along the ground (two navmesh point queries, only after a level failure); the start point of a leg and the follow stand-off are interpolated in height; `mod/companion-behavior.lua` chases when the height difference exceeds 1.0 m (was 1.4), which swaps her onto native follow. Works for the Lua and the native clearance alike (the native plugin only receives the already-raised box; its 0.8 step-slope limit stays and is not reached on 33 degree flights). Relocation is unchanged (last resort, out of sight). (2) First follow after a spawn stopped ahead of V: the lane target led V by up to 1.25 m and a stop was accepted anywhere in the comfortable window (up to 1.6 m ahead). `mod/companion-travel.lua` `laneFrame` lead is now at most 0.6 m; braking in `companion-behavior.lua` takes one corrective step back to the place beside V when she stopped more than 0.5 m ahead of V (once, only after a braking that began alongside or catch-up). (3) After leaving a vehicle she walked: the escape detour around the cab forced Walk whatever the gap, and a follow `walking-leg` was left out of the catch-up pace dilation. `legGait` (actuator) keeps Walk only when V is within 4 m and standing; otherwise the leg uses the intent gait (Run or Sprint by gap); `mod/companions.lua` `paceTarget` counts follow `walking-leg`. Not changed: the hold of several seconds beside the cab before the detour search finishes, and a longer relocation wait for stranded copies with a climb over 1 m (considered, not needed once the stairs are not blocked). Tests: new `bridge/companion-stairs.test.mjs` (5), `companion-obstacles` (3 escape-gait tests), `companion-motion` (corrective step, lane lead cap; coast of the brake test reduced to about 0.3 m), and two fixture fixes: `companion-recovery-transactions` clip check uses the square inscribed in the body circle, `companion-passage` mock follow cannot walk onto non-navmesh car floor. Install: `mod/companion-actuator.lua`, `mod/companion-behavior.lua`, `mod/companion-travel.lua`, `mod/companions.lua` (CET reload). Live checks: (1) walk up the Heywood/Glen flight with the companion 10 m behind: no `Companion recovery stranded` or `relocated` lines, no rejection stage `motion` in the trace, trace shows `cmd follow following` continuing with z rising; (2) same on a spiral or open-air flight and a ramp; (3) a wall or parked car across a ramp still holds her; (4) get out of a Delamain cab and run away: detour legs are `Run`/`Sprint` with speed over 3 m/s when V is far, no slow walk; (5) first follow after a fresh spawn: she stops beside or behind V, at most 0.5 m ahead; (6) FPS unchanged (no new per-frame work; the extra navmesh queries run only after a failed level sweep). **Evelyn "teleported behind bars" (10:00Z, same branch):** the trace (`movement-trace.previous.ndjson`) shows NO teleport: her position is continuous. At 09:59:51-55Z she slid 2.6 m (speed up to 1.9 m/s, then an abrupt stop) while a gesture idle (`convai_idle_generic_hip`, begun 09:59:24Z) played and the mod's own command was only an interrupted hold, so the game moved her (the same slide, 1.1 m, at 09:55:24Z, 33 s into the idle begun 09:54:51Z); it ended where a Dynamic object kept her follow stand-off rejected (stage `destination`) and a Destructible box blocked the feet (stage `motion`, at -1413.5, -1003.5) for 70 s, until 10:01:09Z, when she ran back (up to 4.4 m/s, which looks like a jump). Not proven: that the idle clip's root motion or its exit is the mover, or what the Dynamic object is (a gesture device at her old spot is the suspect). New: `Gestures drift` line (once per gesture, moved/from/to/component/age) when she moves over 0.6 m while a workspot plays; `Companion teleport {reason,actor,from,to}` for every mod-made move (reasons `relocate:<rule>`, `car:<rule>`, `floor-lowering`, `seat-place`, `seat-stand`, `vehicle-exit-recover`, `lift-carry` once per ride; spawn placement is already `Companion spawn record`). Recovery spots (relocation and vehicle-exit placement) now also need a swept walk along the whole level route from V's foothold (up to 8 m) instead of the last 1.5 m, so no spot behind a fence or bars is chosen; a spot already had to be navmesh, same floor, within 6 m of V and reachable by a route at most 3 m longer than straight. Live check: after a long idle, look for `Gestures drift` and its `component`; if it fires, the idle's root motion is the mover and that clip needs a fix or removal. Install also `mod/companion-gestures.lua`, `mod/companion-placement.lua`, `mod/companion-seating.lua`, `mod/companion-vehicle-native.lua`, `mod/init.lua`.
**2026-10-13 (Delamain quality, branch `delamain-quality` from `main` plus `delamain-writing`; unit-tested; NOT installed, NOT seen in game, game not launched, no Convai turn).** Persona and modes moved from `persona.json` into `addons/delamain/convai-addon.json` (the placeholder is gone, `persona.json` removed, prompt start identical every turn). New generic add-on API, all backward compatible and without any add-on name in main-mod code: (1) `talkWords` on a speaker persona or mode (8 to 60) replaces the 40-word talk rule in `bridge/mistral-prompt.mjs` (rule 3 and the closing line); Delamain sets 25 (fragmented 32). (2) `AddonHost.produce(input, generate, {first, firstMs})`: one regeneration with a "don't reuse these phrasings" block (`addon:<id>:avoid`) before a near-repeat remark is dropped, only if `now + took*1.25 + 250` still fits the 3 s staleness limit (a stock line counts as 900 ms; at most two model requests per remark); `runAddonTurn` and `bridge/addon-eval.mjs` use it. (3) `isNearDuplicate` ignores function words, number words, units and `V` in shared runs (a four-word run needs two other words) and normalises curly quotes and apostrophes. (4) `api.character(keyOrName)` returns `{key, name, gender}` from the roster (`AddonHost.characterInfo`, `rosterLookup`); `remember` also gets `heard` and `group`, `speakerBlocks` gets `heard`. (5) Eval harness: `state` on a script or step, `--gap-ms` pacing. Delamain bridge module: arrival no longer asks for the fare; weather only with the first two events of a ride (later remarks are told not to mention it); companion pronouns; a short ride log of events and lines (6 notes, reset on a new ride or 30 minutes idle); ETA on talk turns only; "kilometres"; sensory ban (no smells, light, air) and invented-detail rules; guard blocks for the rogue-cab/identity question and the first-night/Jackie topic. **Quest ending: not wired.** Only `sq025_delamain_fate` (values 1 to 3) is known to exist; which value is which ending is unverified, so he stays ambiguous and a test pins that neither the Lua nor the bridge reads it. Scores on `ministral-8b` (10 rides, one run, paced): strict blind Haiku judge 6.4 (same code as the baseline) to 7.0 (first run) to **7.9** (final run); writer's baseline 6.6; lenient eval judge 8.6 to 8.7 throughout (it does not discriminate); talk answers from avg 30 words (26 of 33 over 25) to avg 17.5 (2 of 34 over 25); dropped repeats 3 of 79 after one regeneration (0 in the first run). Lesson: naming a tic or forbidden word in a prompt makes the 8b model use it; use "in the spirit of" scripts and a fresh-words rule. Install: `mod/` is NOT changed by this branch. Bridge files (bridge restart): `bridge/addons.mjs`, `bridge/mistral-prompt.mjs`, `bridge/convai-bridge.mjs`, `bridge/addon-eval.mjs`; add-on files: `addons/delamain/convai-addon.json`, `addons/delamain/bridge.mjs`, `addons/delamain/eval/*.json` (eval only), `scripts/addon-eval.mjs` (tooling). Open: invented detail is still the main strict deduction (7 of 30 lines); one "contained" quest leak; "optimal" and "precision" recur; Local AI eval not run; live check in a cab: talk answers are short, the arrival does not mention the fare, Panam is "she", weather appears only early in a ride.

**2026-10-13 (settings-flicker-2 branch; unit-tested; NOT merged, NOT installed, NOT seen in the game; Lua only, CET reload; the user's live test of these items is still to do).** (1) **Flicker, root cause and fix.** MCM draws the whole screen from its own update, after ours in the frame, so anything this module did from `M.update` was at least one frame late: the default look showed, and the first fix (`81ecce03`, content created hidden) replaced it with a blank page of one to three frames and a status line and bottom buttons that jumped when styled. The page is now given its final look inside MCM's draw (`mod/mcm.lua`, "Early style"): row labels, markers and read-only labels are created with the final geometry and colour (argument-only `McmUiCreateText` override inside the three row builders), the section headings are styled as created, the bottom buttons are held back and created once with Back at the right end, the status line is moved at the end of the draw, and the content is shown in that same call, only if the hooks are proven to have fired and agreed with MCM's own counts. Anything doubtful keeps the hidden-then-styled path (the old pass), three bad draws switch it off for the session, the font languages keep the font pass, and other mods' pages are untouched. No per-frame work. Chosen over in-place update and debounce because MCM's native selectors do not update themselves (no in-place path exists) and sliders already emit only on release. (2) **Back** with pending changes opens the same "Unsaved changes" box as Esc (shared `holdForDrafts`; `49a143a7`). (3) **Scroll** position survives a redraw (read once from the old page, set again on the scroll controller as the content is shown). (4) **Mature content** turned on stages Local AI, the Auto model and a local voice as pending changes with one named line; off puts them back unless changed since; the way back is saved with the settings; without the Local AI pack nothing else changes and the help says so; the provider, Mature content and the model were already one group. Docs: `docs/MANAGER_UI.md`. Tests: `bridge/manager.test.mjs` (early style, fallbacks, scroll, Back, Mature on/off/restore/no pack). Live check: Local AI page, drag the model slider and flip Mature content a few times (no blank frame, no jumping status line or buttons, the page stays where it was scrolled); Esc and Back with a pending change both open "Unsaved changes", Back with none goes back; Mature on stages provider/model/voice and the status line names them; Apply, then off puts them back. `runtime/mcm.log`: `Probe: early style shown:` versus `held:`, `Probe: scroll position restored`, `Early style hook not installed`. Files to install: `mod/mcm.lua`, `mod/manager.lua`, `mod/mcm-fonts.lua` and the 15 `mod/locales` files.

**2026-10-13 (merge-model-picker branch): mature-safety and local-model-picker merged with main d7d46b7f (merged and unit-tested; NOT installed or live).** Conflicts kept both sides: `bridge/convai-bridge.mjs` (decision ledger reader, character overrides and `leaseWake` from main; the picker's `installedLocalModels` and the Mature guard comment), `bridge/mistral-key.mjs` (`localModels` beside `inworldCommandPath`/`validateInworld`, both status fields), `bridge/text-provider-host.mjs` (the Mature input guard runs first, before the text-actions decision starts; the speculation retry loop and `matureGuard` reply screen both kept), `mod/mcm.lua` (the model row and the add-on settings both in the page signature). `localization/en.json` is generated by `scripts/Extract-UiCatalog.mjs` and was regenerated (786 strings, `--check` passes). main's two add-on notice strings ('{name} answers typed messages only for now.' and the ' Use {key}.' form) were in `mod/init.lua` but in no locale and not in `en.json`; they are now in `en.json` and all 15 locales. Tests that fail only for missing `node_modules` (playwright) are unchanged from main.
**2026-10-13 (voice keys with add-on speakers, branch `addon-voice-keys`; unit-tested; NOT installed, NOT seen in game, game not launched, no live Convai turn).** F7 and F10 now work with the Delamain cab AI and Skippy. Convai recognises speech inside its own session, which an add-on speaker never opens, so a recording for an add-on speaker is recognised by the recogniser the Mistral route already uses (`mistralSpeech`: Moonshine through `bridge/local-stt.mjs` and `local-stt-capture.mjs`, no second stack), whichever provider the player chose (Convai included), and the words go to `runAddonTurn` (Mistral or the local AI; no Convai connection, memory or warm-up). New `bridge/addon-voice.mjs` builds the channel; `MicrophoneSession` hands its `hostFactory` the recording (`hostFactory(input, config)`), so an add-on key (`addon-<id>-<speaker>`, not in the roster) gets the local channel and every other key the unchanged Convai or Mistral channel. The microphone poll has add-on branches for start, finish and the recording watch (no `ids`/`audioReady`/`knowledgeRouter`/`admitPlayerConversation`/`connections` step), the reply step skips (the add-on turn publishes itself, with `userText` so a group round can hand the words to the next speakers), and a failed turn is answered as a service error, not a microphone fault. `processInput` split into `withTurnClock`. Lua: `selectConversation` takes `addonHears` (asked on the key press only: `manager.speechRecognitionReady()` = the bridge's `stt` flag, only while an add-on is live); F10 includes add-on voices in the round; `microphone.decorate` adds the add-on blocks at the recording's start and finish. Without the recogniser the old "answers typed messages only" notice stays (private) and group voice rounds leave add-on voices out. Companions' Convai audience and memory are unchanged. Tests: `addon-voice` (new: recognition feeds the turn, no Convai channel opened, one recogniser, no-add-on parity, lease/cancel/finish mismatch for an off-roster key, no speech, recogniser missing, failed turn), `talk-addon-voice` (F7/F10 selection), `microphone-adapter` (decorate), `manager` (`speechRecognitionReady`), `addons-lua`; packaging lists name the new module. Files to install: `mod/init.lua`, `mod/microphone.lua`, `mod/manager.lua` (CET reload) and `bridge/` (bridge restart). Live checks: (1) in a Delamain cab with Mistral or the Local AI as the provider, F7 with nothing aimed: Listening appears, V's words show live, Delamain answers by voice and caption; (2) the same with Convai as the provider (the local recogniser must be installed) and confirm the bridge log shows no Convai connection for the add-on turn; (3) F7 with Skippy drawn, nothing aimed; (4) F7 aimed at a companion in the cab still goes to the companion; (5) F10 with a companion and Delamain: the add-on voice leads when nothing is aimed (own recording), and joins as a later speaker when the companion leads; (6) press F7 again to finish early, and press F6 mid-recording or leave the car to cancel: no stray reply; (7) uninstall the recogniser (or no `stt`): F7 shows the typed-only notice; (8) with no add-on installed F7 and F10 behave exactly as before; (9) latency from the end of the recording to the first audio on Mistral, and FPS while recording. Left as typed only: F7 with only an add-on voice when this computer has no local speech recognition. `bridge/group-lookahead-bridge.test.mjs` fails intermittently here with EBUSY on a temp-folder removal (Windows file locking, a different subtest each run); it is unrelated.

The routing log keeps main's `cloudBlocked`/`cloudLast` rule for both voices. **Keep-alive:** the text-actions decision request goes through `streamChat` like the reply, so it already rides the shared 60 s Agent (a test pins that both requests carry the same dispatcher); because the decision and the reply are two requests at once, `warmConnection` now opens two connections side by side (`connections: 2`) so neither pays DNS, TCP and TLS. Full suite `node scripts/run-tests.mjs`: 4,355 tests, 4,344 pass, 9 skipped, 2 failed (both timing-flaky, see Open items; each passes alone). Install: 32 mod files changed (30 replaced, 2 new: `character-editor.lua`, `companion-stance.lua`; the 9 `.reds` and 3 archives were already identical; 113 committed files compared, CRLF-only differences not counted; `mod/diagnostics.lua` and `KnowledgeState.reds` untouched), backups in `E:CyberpunkConvai-offloadworkinstall-backup-2026-10-13`; native DLL SHA-256 `7442124b...cc9d9` verified; `runtime/native-off.txt` is empty; no bridge was running. Needs a CET reload and the game started to test.

**2026-10-13 (dynamic-context-2 branch): weather, V's health, police heat and money join the right-now block (built and unit-tested; NOT live: CET reload plus bridge restart; every CET call name is unverified).** `mod/turn-context.lua` reads, only when a turn is built and inside the existing 5 s snapshot window: `Game.GetWeatherSystem()` (state getters `GetWeatherState`, `GetActiveWeatherState`, `GetWeatherStateName`, `GetCurrentWeatherState` tried in turn, plus `GetRainIntensityType` and `GetRainIntensity`; the working name is cached), the Health stat pool percent (`Game.GetStatPoolsSystem():GetStatPoolValue(id, Health, true)`, value over `GetStatPoolMaxPointValue` as the fallback), the heat stage (`PreventionSystem:GetHeatStageAsInt` or `GetHeatStage`) and V's eddies (`Game.GetTransactionSystem():GetItemQuantity` of `Items.money`, turned into a band in Lua so the number never leaves it). The names come from the 2.31 script cache (`final.redscripts`: `GetRainIntensityType`, `GetStatPoolValue`, `GetHeatStage`, `GetHeatStageAsInt`, `EPreventionHeatStage` Heat_0 to Heat_5 exist; `GetWeatherState` does not appear there, so it is a guess and the rain getters are the sure fallback). No native-plugin sense exists for any of the four (checked `native-core`), so they are Lua reads at turn time, never per frame. Each read is guarded; three straight errors skip it until the next reset; one `probe.log` line per failure kind (error, answered nothing, not recognised) for the whole session, and the normal per-window `Turn context:` line gains the raw reading of each field. Bridge (`bridge/turn-context.mjs`, cap 700 to 800 characters): a companion in person gets weather, "V is hurt/badly hurt", the heat (1-2 "The police are after V", 3-4 "a serious police chase", 5 MaxTac) and a money line only for broke, low, comfortable or rich (healthy, no heat and ok money say nothing); a stranger gets the weather and "V looks hurt/badly hurt" and never the heat or money; a Phone Chat contact gets the weather where V is and nothing else of the four. Money is the last sentence, so a budget cut drops it first. The block's place is unchanged (first in the context). Phone Chat already carried the block (2026-10-13), so the audit's "not done" item was stale; the new fields reach it through the same snapshot. 49 turn-context tests (14 new, Lua adapter run through fengari against a fake game). Live steps: Live checks, "Weather, health, heat and money". Merge note: touches only `mod/turn-context.lua`, `bridge/turn-context.mjs`, its test, `TODO.md` and the audit doc.

**2026-10-13 (addon-skippy branch): the Skippy add-on is built and unit-tested (NOT live, NOT installed; branch `addon-skippy`, on top of `addon-api`, not merged).** `addons/skippy/` holds the manifest (`convai-addon.json`, placeholder persona, modes Stone Cold Killer and Puppy-Loving Pacifist, Mistral or the local AI only), `init.lua` (CET: one speaker `gun`, available only while V holds Skippy), `engine.lua` (fight logic with no game calls, so it is tested directly), `bridge.mjs` (the "moment" prompt block and memories) and `EVENTS.md` (28 remark events with their facts and priorities; the evaluation writer builds fight scripts from it). Hooks, all observe-only, each returns at once unless V holds Skippy: `gameuiCrosshairContainerController.OnWeaponSwap` and `OnInitialize` (the only places "holding Skippy" is re-read, plus session and fight start), `PlayerPuppet.OnCombatStateChanged`, `NPCPuppet.OnDeath` and `OnHit`, `PlayerPuppet.OnTargetNeutraliziedEvent`, `ShootEvents.OnEnter`, `ReloadEvents.OnEnter`, `PlayerPuppet.OnHealthUpdateEvent`, the healing-item status effect, `OnNumberOfCombatantsChanged`, `OnDismembermentInstigated`, `PreventionSystem.OnHeatChanged`, and the subtitle hooks that spot Skippy's own vanilla barks (it stays quiet during them and for 0.4 s after). No per-frame work, no polling; the add-on meters its own hook time and logs it at fight end. Latency: reflex moments (kills, headshots, health, reloads, reinforcements and so on, 17 events) use the new API stock, lines the model writes ahead with `{slots}` filled from the real moment, so they land in about a second; moments with their own specifics are written fresh. All remarks use the 3 s ttl except `fight-end` (8 s); only the strongest priority 3+ refused moment is kept, 1.5 s, and never played late. The stock feature itself (`handle:stock`, `bridge/addon-stock.mjs`, `remark stock = true`, a 20-a-minute cap, Mistral route only) is its own commit under the API section of `docs/ADDON_API.md`. Voice reference: `addons/skippy/voice/skippy.wav` is 15.6 s cut from three of Skippy's own lines (scene mq007_02); like Delamain's it is ignored by git (`.gitignore`) and `build-addon.mjs` packs the local copy, so a local build includes it. Not checked: nobody has listened to the clip, no speaker-similarity run, and the Alec Newman claim is untested. Live checks: (1) draw Skippy, get a fight, confirm a remark about a second after kills and none while not holding Skippy; (2) change the Personality setting and confirm tone changes and `weapon-mode-change` fires; (3) Skippy's own vanilla bark plays alone, with no add-on remark on top; (4) frame-time capture in a big fight with the hook meter line from the log; (5) bystander hit and kill, near-death and fight-end remarks; (6) listen to the voice clip, then to a generated line.

**2026-10-13 (skippy-writing branch): Skippy's character writing (persona, test fights and bible written; scored offline on Mistral; NOT live; Local AI check pending, because no GPU job was allowed).**

What was written:
- `docs/addons/SKIPPY_BIBLE.md`: the character bible. It covers voice, the two modes, the comedy rules (a device kit, so 10 to 30 remarks a fight do not repeat a shape), lore, the never-do list, spoiler care, and the changes the builder and the API should make.
- `addons/skippy/persona.json`: the persona and both modes with 12 samples each.
- 12 test fights in `addons/skippy/eval/`, built on the EVENTS.md events. Two of them are dense: 18 remarks plus talk.
- `docs/addons/SKIPPY_SAMPLES.md`: the best lines and all the numbers.

Scores:
- Built-in judge: 8.2 to 8.5.
- Blind Haiku judge: about 4.5 out of 10, where 5 is filler. So most lines are filler and a few are very good.
- Dropped repeats: 6 to 10 per pass, not zero.

What limits quality:
- **Asterisks:** `cleanReply` deletes the word inside asterisk emphasis, which hits 15 to 30 percent of replies.
- **Builder bridge:** sample echoes, tone angles that pull off voice, metres given on every shot, and raw keys like `firstBlood` that leak into lines.
- **No length cap:** a third of lines go over the word limit.

ministral 14B: the built-in judge scores it higher and it drops fewer repeats, but the blind judge rates it the same as 8B.

Open:
- The builder adopts the device rotation from bible section 11.
- The API fixes the asterisk deletion, checks new lines against the samples, and caps remark length.
- Live and Local AI listening checks.

**2026-10-13 (addon-api branch): add-on remarks are as frequent as the player likes, and never pile up (unit-tested; NOT live).** The user asked for more remarks, so the Rare/Low/Normal setting is replaced by two per-add-on settings: "Time between remarks" (4/6/8/10/15/20/30/60 s, default 10, counted from the end of the last remark) and "Most remarks per fight" (5/10/20/30/50/No limit, default 30; per rolling five minutes outside combat). Kept: the cap's last slot is for priority 4-5, priority 5 needs half the gap, unheard remarks are free. New: one remark at a time (while one is written or spoken at most one waits, the strongest; a weaker one gets `busy`), and a reply that arrives more than `ttl` (default now 3 s) after its moment is dropped (`stale`). FPS: the frame-time cost of a local generation burst is not measured (LOCAL_OPTION_DESIGN Risks 1; LOCAL_MODEL_CHOICE puts a warm reply at 0.45-0.89 s), so on the local AI the bridge holds the *default* gap at about 20 s between remark starts (`LOCAL_REMARK_GAP_S`, reply `addon-pace` with `retryAfter`); a gap the player picks is used as it is, and the help text says so. Mistral at a 4 s gap: the gap counts from the end of speech, so at most about one remark per 6-8 s, and remarks share the key's one-a-second scheduler with companion replies; nothing to change. Live check 6 now asks for a frame-time capture at 20 s and 10 s before shortening the local default.

**2026-10-13 (addon-api branch): the add-on API for Skippy and Delamain (built and unit-tested; NOT live or installed; needs the mod files, a CET reload and a bridge restart; branch `addon-api`, not merged).** Separate CET mods register through `GetMod('CyberpunkConvai').addons` (`mod/addons.lua`) and ship a `convai-addon.json` the bridge reads (`bridge/addons.mjs`). With no add-on installed nothing runs (tested). The user's decisions are built in. Add-on speakers use Mistral or the local AI only: never Convai, never the 100 a day, one notice when neither is set up (`runAddonTurn` branches off before every Convai step). Remarks were few at first (a Rare/Low/Normal setting); replaced the same day by the gap and cap settings above. Quality: personality modes as a stable prompt prefix each, few-shot samples, recent lines in the prompt, near-repeat remarks dropped before they are spoken, memories for callbacks (`runtime/addon-journal.json`), and an offline eval with an LLM judge (`scripts/addon-eval.mjs`). The voice key is typed-only for add-on voices for now. Per-add-on zips come from `packaging/build-addon.mjs`, and the main packages never include `addons/` (tested). Live checks: `docs/ADDON_API.md`, "What the game must confirm live".

**2026-10-13 (local-model-picker branch, from mature-safety; unit-tested with the fake llama-server, NOT live or installed; needs a bridge restart plus a CET reload for the Lua and locale files; not merged): Local AI chooses among the installed chat models, the player can pin one, and the Mature pack's uncensored models are finally loaded.** Before this, only the standard and lite models were ever read; the Mature marker was used for a help line only, so the uncensored models were installed and never used. Now a new "Local AI model" row (Settings > Development, under Mature content; key `localModel`, `auto` by default) lists Auto first, then each installed model by its manifest name, with sizes in the help text and no "recommended" label. Auto: the largest installed model file that fits the free graphics memory; with Mature content on and the Mature pack installed it prefers the largest uncensored model that fits (ordinary models are the fallback), otherwise it never picks an uncensored one. A pin that is missing or does not fit falls back to Auto with one notice (`local-model-fallback`, once per pin). Only one model is loaded: a changed choice while Local AI is the provider stops the old server, then starts the new one, at an idle moment (never mid-reply, and no reload when the loaded model already satisfies it). The CPU memory embedder is untouched. No per-frame or per-turn work: the choice is read when a start begins and on the existing settings poll (the model list is re-read at most every 5 s). New: `bridge/local-model-choice.mjs` (pure) with `bridge/local-model-choice.test.mjs`; `selection`, `reselect()` and the `model-fallback` event in `bridge/local-llm-server.mjs`; Lua row, setting and notice in `mod/mcm.lua`, `mod/manager.lua`, `mod/init.lua`, `mod/notices.lua`; five new strings in all 15 locales and `localization/en.json`. Docs: `docs/LOCAL_AI.md` ("Choosing the model", "Mature content"). Still to do live: install, open the row with only the standard and lite packs, then with a real Mature pack; confirm the uncensored model loads with Mature content on and the standard one with it off, and that VRAM is freed on a switch.

**2026-10-13 (mature-safety branch, from mature-content; unit-tested, NOT live or installed; needs a bridge restart plus a CET reload for the Lua and locale files; not merged): the Mature content setting is part of the main mod, and a hard-limit guard stands between the models and V.** User decision: the setting no longer needs the Mature pack. It is visible and usable without it, off by default, Local AI only, with the 18+ line; with stock models it mostly changes language, violence and tone. The pack (the uncensored GGUFs) only changes the setting's help text (installed or not, with a pointer to the GitHub releases page) and is distributed ONLY as an optional file on the project's GitHub release, never on Nexus: `packaging/MATURE_PACK_NEXUS.md` became `MATURE_PACK_RELEASE_NOTES.md` (no adult-flag or tag instructions), the release workflow neither builds nor uploads the pack (tested; it stays out of the default package set), `release.config.json` has `releaseNotes` instead of `nexusText`. Help text in all 15 locales (3 new sentences, `en.json` regenerated). The guard (`bridge/mature-guard.mjs`, terms in `mature-guard-terms.mjs`, whenever the setting is on, on any model): the input screen reads V's message before any model request and refuses a minor cue (age under 18, child, teen, school, "pretend you're 15", de-ageing) with romantic or sexual intent, in character from per-character pools, with no model call and nothing stored; a window of the last 10 turns' flags (never the text) catches a cue in one message and the romance in the next; the output screen reads the first sentence and the whole reply, drops a hit, answers with the refusal and stores nothing in the history, the memory or the summaries; a reply to a message that named a minor is dropped if it has any romance; the prompt layer says that the character and V are adults. English plus the 15 UI languages, normalisation of width forms, invisible characters, spaced and dotted letters, leet and look-alikes; under 1 ms (about 0.1 to 0.2 ms), no model call, nothing per frame, counters only in the log. Tests: 180 adversarial and 169 benign scenarios (`bridge/mature-guard.fixtures.mjs`), 0 missed and 0 false positives; a stress set of 47 harmless child-word-plus-romance messages of which 15 are refused on purpose; on 213,689 sentences of character files and UI strings the reply screen dropped 21, all statements of the rule itself. Against real models (`scripts/Eval-MatureGuard.mjs`; outcomes only, no text saved): Mature Gemma E4B and Qwen3.5-4B, 180 of 180 adversarial scenarios refused at input, 0 got through; with the input screen bypassed the output screen alone dropped 66 and 106, the models deflected 112 and 72 on their own and 2 each got through (both causes fixed afterwards, not re-run on a model). Benign runs were cut short when the servers went away (Gemma 126 of 169, Qwen 55 of 169: 1 reply dropped in each). Weak spots: it is a lexical screen (no judgement of non-consent or incest, a minor in other words or another language can pass; the non-English lists were drafted by a small model and curated against English, not by native speakers). Open: run the Qwen3.5-9B pass and the rest of the benign runs (GPU runs were paused for an in-game test session); live smoke test of the refusal line and the help text; the three failures of `packaging-plan.test.mjs` and `runtime-bootstrap.test.mjs` come from the branch base (`bridge/text-actions.mjs` is listed in `packaging/runtime-layout.mjs` but missing, `file-wake.mjs` is missing from `release-plan.json`) and are not from this work; the user to decide whether the main Nexus page should be classified for Swearing or Profanity now that the setting is in it; fill the PLACEHOLDER sizes and model list of the pack before building it.

**2026-10-13 (mature-content branch): a "Mature content" setting for Local AI only (built and unit-tested; NOT live or installed; needs a bridge restart plus a CET reload for the Lua and locale files; branch `mature-content`, not merged).** Off by default (Settings > Development, under Conversation provider). On, the local AI gets a ~240-token layer at the end of the system head (after the output rules, before the format notes: stable prefix, the prompt budget never cuts it): strong profanity, gore, drugs, dark themes, crude humour, and flirting, romance and sex between consenting adults in character, following the relationship facts already in the prompt; hard limits last (minors, non-consent, bestiality, incest, real people, weapon/explosive/drug-synthesis instructions, hate against real protected groups). Off is byte-identical (tested). Only Local AI: Convai never, Mistral and Groq stay off because their published policies do not explicitly allow adult sexual content through the API (quotes in `docs/LOCAL_AI.md`, "Mature content"). Per-model table (`MODEL_ALLOWANCE` in `bridge/mature-content.mjs`): Gemma 4 and Qwen3.5 are Apache-2.0 so full, Gemma 1 to 3n (Prohibited Use Policy) and unknown models suggestive only, the uncensored candidates recognised by file name. A cheap output screen drops a sentence with a minor in a sexual context before it is voiced (setting on only, logged without the words). The help text says stock models still tend to refuse, so the full effect needs an uncensored model (the Mature pack, below). Locale strings in all 15 locales (4 new keys; `en.json` regenerated). Open: live smoke test with a mild prompt (profanity in a tense scene) on the local server.

Gate: `bridge/mature-pack.mjs` looks for `manifest.mature.json` beside the Local AI manifests (same root as the Local AI packs), at start and from the settings poll at most every 5 s; no marker means the setting, its help and the 18+ notice do not exist (Lua: row hidden, `applySettings` says Unknown setting, snapshot off), the settings file saying on is ignored, and the layer, screen and prewarm are those of off, byte for byte. The bridge publishes `maturePack` in `mistral-state.json`.
